At 0.50s the ball and block have contacted the pillows, but by 0.75s both are already airborne again, and by 1.25s they are near the hanging grabbers. supported collision ⏱ 0.5s, 0.75s, 1.25s, 2.25s, 2.5s, 2.75s, 3.25s, 4.75s, 5s, 5.25s, 5.75s

A tennis ball might bounce somewhat, but not repeatedly to grabber height from a soft pillow, and a rigid block on a pillow should mostly sink and settle rather than bounce high and keep oscillating.
